Licensing Service Plan Reference

20 August 2026Doc update+20 / −14 lines

In brief

The reference was updated August 19, 2026, adding entries for several Dynamics 365 and Microsoft 365 plans and refreshing listed Microsoft 365 licensing rows.

What Entra admins need to know

Administrators mapping licenses to product or service-plan identifiers should use the updated table or CSV when working with these plans.

This editorial summary was generated by AI from the documentation changes. Verify important details in the full Microsoft Learn article.
